---
> # GENERATED by bench/core/gen_campaign.py — campaign 'deep-context'. Resumable: rerun to continue
> # after an interruption (per-probe markers in <campaign>/tmp/done/). Edit the spec and
> # regenerate. Behaviour baked in (no hand-editing needed):
> #   * CONTINUE-ON-FAILURE: a server that fails to start is recorded and the run moves on.
> #   * PRE-FLIGHT VRAM GUARD: a server whose predicted VRAM > VRAM_BUDGET_MIB is SKIPPED
> #     before launch → GPU/VRAM-only, never spills to system RAM (no GTT thrash / freeze).
> #   * SUBSET / SHORT runs: ONLY='glob,..' picks probes; REPS=/MAX_TOKENS= shorten a pass.
> #   * TWO ROOTS: raw (per-probe run dirs, GPU CSVs, done/ markers) -> <campaign>/tmp/; results
> #     (results.jsonl, meta/failures/skipped, server-<name>.txt, charts, appendix) -> <campaign>/results/.

< EB="$ROOT/bench/engine-bench"
< GEN="$ROOT/bench/workloads/generated"

> CAMPAIGN_DIR="${CAMPAIGN_DIR:-$HERE}"
> mkdir -p "$CAMPAIGN_DIR" && CAMPAIGN_DIR="$(cd "$CAMPAIGN_DIR" && pwd)"

> GEN="$ROOT/bench/workloads/tmp"

> # --- run-time knobs (override on the command line) ---

> ONLY="${ONLY:-}"                 # comma-separated slug globs; empty = all probes
> REPS_OVERRIDE="${REPS:-}"        # override every probe's reps (quick pass)
> MAXTOK_OVERRIDE="${MAX_TOKENS:-}"  # override every probe's max_tokens
> mkdir -p "$RAW/done"

<   local s; for s in "$@"; do [ -f "$CAMPAIGN_DIR/done/$s" ] || return 0; done; return 1; }
---
>   local s; for s in "$@"; do [ -f "$RAW/done/$s" ] || return 0; done; return 1; }
> want_probe () {  # honor ONLY='glob,glob' filter
>   [ -z "$ONLY" ] && return 0
>   local g; IFS="," read -ra _globs <<<"$ONLY"
>   for g in "${_globs[@]}"; do case "$1" in $g) return 0;; esac; done; return 1; }
> probes_selected () {  # 0 if ONLY matches >=1 of this server's probes (don't load a model
>   local s; for s in "$@"; do want_probe "$s" && return 0; done; return 1; }  # for filtered-out probes
> guard_ok () {  # name predicted_mib -> 0 if within budget (or unknown), else record SKIPPED
>   local name=$1 pred=$2
>   [ "$pred" -lt 0 ] && return 0   # no prediction available (spec has no vram block)
>   if [ "$pred" -gt "$VRAM_BUDGET_MIB" ]; then
>     echo "SKIPPED (predicted ${pred} MiB > budget ${VRAM_BUDGET_MIB} MiB): $name" \
>       | tee -a "$RES/skipped.txt"; return 1; fi
>   return 0; }
> ensure_fixtures () {  # auto-build any missing prompt fixture from its canonical recipe
>   local WL="$ROOT/bench/workloads" name n k
>   for name in "$@"; do
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] && continue
>     echo ">>> building missing fixture: $name"
>     case "$name" in
>       codereview-*.txt)
>         n="${name#codereview-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/codereview-large.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --src "$WL/corpus/py-rich" \
>           --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       thinking-hard.txt)
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/thinking-hard.prompt.txt" \
>           --target-tokens 0 --out "$GEN/thinking-hard.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*-v*.txt)  # variant set: build the base with enough --variants (writes v1..vK)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%%-v*}"; k="${name##*-v}"; k="${k%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" \
>           --variants "$k" --out "$GEN/agentic-$n.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*.txt)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       *)
>         echo "FIXTURE-UNKNOWN: no build recipe for '$name' — build it by hand (see" \
>              "bench/workloads/README.md) and re-run" >&2; exit 1 ;;
>     esac
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] || { echo "FIXTURE-BUILD-FAILED: $name" >&2; exit 1; }
>   done; }

> start_server () {  # name ctx kv -> 0 up, nonzero (recorded) on failure; run CONTINUES
>   local name=$1 ctx=$2 kv=$3 mtp=$4 np=$5
>   echo ">>> starting $name (ctx=$ctx kv=$kv mtp=$mtp np=$np)"
>   if BACKEND=vulkan CTX="$ctx" NP="$np" KV="$kv" MTP="$mtp" UB=2048 B=4096 FA=on PORT=8081 \
>        "$ROOT/stack/serve/llamacpp/serve_llamacpp.sh" start; then return 0; fi
>   echo "SERVER-FAILED (start OOM/other; expected near the ceiling): $name ctx=$ctx kv=$kv" \

> def roots(D):
>     """Read roots for D: itself first (flat legacy run dir), then the split campaign roots
>     (results/ = graded rows, tmp/ = raw)."""
>     return (D, os.path.join(D, "results"), os.path.join(D, "tmp"))
> def rd(D, name):
>     """Rows of `name` under any of its roots (missing -> [])."""
>     for root in roots(D):
>         p = os.path.join(root, name)
>         if os.path.exists(p):
>             return load_jsonl(p)
>     return []
> def write_gpu_peaks(D):
>     """MEASURED peak VRAM/GTT (MiB) per config label from the tmp/gpu_<label>.csv sample files
>     (iron rule 7: a reported memory number is a MEASURED sample, never a calculation) ->
>     results/gpu_peaks.json. Reuses bench/core/report.py's gpu_peaks (same CSV columns, per-CSV max)
>     instead of re-implementing the sampling; the CSV paths are relative to the campaign dir."""
>     results, tmp = os.path.join(D, "results"), os.path.join(D, "tmp")
>     if not (os.path.isdir(results) and os.path.isdir(tmp)):
>         return                                          # not a split campaign dir -> nothing to write
>     sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(HERE, "..", "core"))
>     from report import gpu_peaks
>     peaks = gpu_peaks(D)
>     if not peaks:
>         return
>     for p in peaks:
>         m = re.search(r"gpu_(.+)\.csv$", p["csv"])
>         if m:
>             p["label"] = m.group(1)
>     ppath = os.path.join(results, "gpu_peaks.json")
>     with open(ppath, "w") as f:
>         json.dump({"generated_by": "bench/quality/make_charts.py",
>                    "provenance": "MEASURED: max over the samples of each GPU sample CSV "
>                                  "(stack/gpu/vram_sampler.py); csv paths relative to the campaign dir",
>                    "runs": peaks}, f, indent=1)
>         f.write("\n")
>     print(f"[gpu] wrote {len(peaks)} measured peak rows -> {ppath}")

< takeaway: ⚠️ CORRECTED — the "64K f16 / 120K q8_0 ceiling" conclusion below is WRONG. Qwen3.6-27B is a HYBRID arch (only 16 of 65 blocks are full-attention; 48 are SSM/linear-attn with no growing KV), so f16 KV is 64 KiB/tok not 260, and the real max is ~240K (f16) / native 262K (q8_0). MEASURED 2026-07-12: 200K f16 loaded in 31s using 29.0 GiB VRAM. The 64K/120K figures were the highest TESTED, never the ceiling. STILL VALID (MEASURED at 40K depth): prefill ~742 tok/s f16 / ~635 q8_0 (−14%), decode flat ~28.5 tok/s, TTFT 54s/63s. Superseded by PATHTOK + hybrid-aware bench/gguf_kv.py.

> takeaway: ⚠️ CORRECTED — the "64K f16 / 120K q8_0 ceiling" conclusion below is WRONG. Qwen3.6-27B is a HYBRID arch (only 16 of 65 blocks are full-attention; 48 are SSM/linear-attn with no growing KV), so f16 KV is 64 KiB/tok not 260, and the real max is ~240K (f16) / native 262K (q8_0). MEASURED 2026-07-12: 200K f16 loaded in 31s using 29.0 GiB VRAM. The 64K/120K figures were the highest TESTED, never the ceiling. STILL VALID (MEASURED at 40K depth): prefill ~742 tok/s f16 / ~635 q8_0 (−14%), decode flat ~28.5 tok/s, TTFT 54s/63s. Superseded by PATHTOK + hybrid-aware bench/core/gguf_kv.py.

< # GENERATED by bench/gen_campaign.py — campaign '27b-maxctx-f16'. Resumable: rerun to continue
< # after an interruption (per-probe markers in <campaign>/done/). Edit the spec and
< # regenerate, or hand-edit this file. Requires the servers' llama.cpp builds present.
< #
< # HAND-EDITED (2026-07-12): this is a max-context BRACKET campaign — servers at/above the VRAM
< # ceiling are EXPECTED to OOM at start. A start failure is therefore recorded to failures.txt and
< # the run CONTINUES to the next server (it does NOT exit), so the f16 OOM cannot halt the q8 arm.
< # A failed server leaves its probe marker absent → it is re-attempted on the next resume (a
< # deterministic OOM just re-fails cheaply and moves on).
---
> # GENERATED by bench/core/gen_campaign.py — campaign '27b-maxctx-f16'. Resumable: rerun to continue
> # after an interruption (per-probe markers in <campaign>/tmp/done/). Edit the spec and
> # regenerate. Behaviour baked in (no hand-editing needed):
> #   * CONTINUE-ON-FAILURE: a server that fails to start is recorded and the run moves on.
> #   * PRE-FLIGHT VRAM GUARD: a server whose predicted VRAM > VRAM_BUDGET_MIB is SKIPPED
> #     before launch → GPU/VRAM-only, never spills to system RAM (no GTT thrash / freeze).
> #   * SUBSET / SHORT runs: ONLY='glob,..' picks probes; REPS=/MAX_TOKENS= shorten a pass.
> #   * TWO ROOTS: raw (per-probe run dirs, GPU CSVs, done/ markers) -> <campaign>/tmp/; results
> #     (results.jsonl, meta/failures/skipped, server-<name>.txt, charts, appendix) -> <campaign>/results/.

< EB="$ROOT/bench/engine-bench"
< GEN="$ROOT/bench/workloads/generated"

> [ -n "$ROOT" ] || { echo "run.sh: cannot find the repo root (git toplevel of $HERE) -- set ROOT=" >&2; exit 1; }
> CAMPAIGN_DIR="${CAMPAIGN_DIR:-$HERE}"
> mkdir -p "$CAMPAIGN_DIR" && CAMPAIGN_DIR="$(cd "$CAMPAIGN_DIR" && pwd)"
> RAW="$CAMPAIGN_DIR/tmp"; RES="$CAMPAIGN_DIR/results"   # raw (ignored) / results (committed)
> mkdir -p "$RAW" "$RES"
> EB="$ROOT/bench/perf/engine-bench"
> GEN="$ROOT/bench/workloads/tmp"

> # --- run-time knobs (override on the command line) ---
> VRAM_BUDGET_MIB="${VRAM_BUDGET_MIB:-32400}"  # guard: skip servers predicted above this
> ONLY="${ONLY:-}"                 # comma-separated slug globs; empty = all probes
> REPS_OVERRIDE="${REPS:-}"        # override every probe's reps (quick pass)
> MAXTOK_OVERRIDE="${MAX_TOKENS:-}"  # override every probe's max_tokens
> mkdir -p "$RAW/done"
> if [ ! -f "$RES/meta.txt" ]; then
>   { echo "date=$(date -Iseconds)"; echo "model=$MODEL"; echo "backend=vulkan"; echo "tuning=ub2048 b4096 faon"; echo "vram_budget_mib=$VRAM_BUDGET_MIB"; } > "$RES/meta.txt"

<   local s; for s in "$@"; do [ -f "$CAMPAIGN_DIR/done/$s" ] || return 0; done; return 1; }
< stop_server () { PORT=8081 "$EB/serve_llamacpp.sh" stop 2>/dev/null || true; }
< start_server () {  # name ctx kv  -> 0 if up, nonzero (recorded) if OOM/failed
<   local name=$1 ctx=$2 kv=$3
<   echo ">>> starting $name (ctx=$ctx kv=$kv)"
<   if BACKEND=vulkan CTX="$ctx" NP=1 KV="$kv" MTP=0 UB=2048 B=4096 FA=on PORT=8081 \
<        "$EB/serve_llamacpp.sh" start; then return 0; fi
<   echo "SERVER-OOM/FAILED (expected bracket ceiling): $name ctx=$ctx kv=$kv" \
<     | tee -a "$CAMPAIGN_DIR/failures.txt"
<   stop_server; return 1; }
---
>   local s; for s in "$@"; do [ -f "$RAW/done/$s" ] || return 0; done; return 1; }
> want_probe () {  # honor ONLY='glob,glob' filter
>   [ -z "$ONLY" ] && return 0
>   local g; IFS="," read -ra _globs <<<"$ONLY"
>   for g in "${_globs[@]}"; do case "$1" in $g) return 0;; esac; done; return 1; }
> probes_selected () {  # 0 if ONLY matches >=1 of this server's probes (don't load a model
>   local s; for s in "$@"; do want_probe "$s" && return 0; done; return 1; }  # for filtered-out probes
> guard_ok () {  # name predicted_mib -> 0 if within budget (or unknown), else record SKIPPED
>   local name=$1 pred=$2
>   [ "$pred" -lt 0 ] && return 0   # no prediction available (spec has no vram block)
>   if [ "$pred" -gt "$VRAM_BUDGET_MIB" ]; then
>     echo "SKIPPED (predicted ${pred} MiB > budget ${VRAM_BUDGET_MIB} MiB): $name" \
>       | tee -a "$RES/skipped.txt"; return 1; fi
>   return 0; }
> ensure_fixtures () {  # auto-build any missing prompt fixture from its canonical recipe
>   local WL="$ROOT/bench/workloads" name n k
>   for name in "$@"; do
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] && continue
>     echo ">>> building missing fixture: $name"
>     case "$name" in
>       codereview-*.txt)
>         n="${name#codereview-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/codereview-large.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --src "$WL/corpus/py-rich" \
>           --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       thinking-hard.txt)
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/thinking-hard.prompt.txt" \
>           --target-tokens 0 --out "$GEN/thinking-hard.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*-v*.txt)  # variant set: build the base with enough --variants (writes v1..vK)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%%-v*}"; k="${name##*-v}"; k="${k%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" \
>           --variants "$k" --out "$GEN/agentic-$n.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*.txt)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       *)
>         echo "FIXTURE-UNKNOWN: no build recipe for '$name' — build it by hand (see" \
>              "bench/workloads/README.md) and re-run" >&2; exit 1 ;;
>     esac
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] || { echo "FIXTURE-BUILD-FAILED: $name" >&2; exit 1; }
>   done; }

> stop_server () { PORT=8081 "$ROOT/stack/serve/llamacpp/serve_llamacpp.sh" stop 2>/dev/null || true; }
> start_server () {  # name ctx kv -> 0 up, nonzero (recorded) on failure; run CONTINUES
>   local name=$1 ctx=$2 kv=$3 mtp=$4 np=$5
>   echo ">>> starting $name (ctx=$ctx kv=$kv mtp=$mtp np=$np)"
>   if BACKEND=vulkan CTX="$ctx" NP="$np" KV="$kv" MTP="$mtp" UB=2048 B=4096 FA=on PORT=8081 \
>        "$ROOT/stack/serve/llamacpp/serve_llamacpp.sh" start; then return 0; fi
>   echo "SERVER-FAILED (start OOM/other; expected near the ceiling): $name ctx=$ctx kv=$kv" \
>     | tee -a "$RES/failures.txt"; stop_server; return 1; }

> ensure_fixtures () {  # auto-build any missing prompt fixture from its canonical recipe
>   local WL="$ROOT/bench/workloads" name n k
>   for name in "$@"; do
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] && continue
>     echo ">>> building missing fixture: $name"
>     case "$name" in
>       codereview-*.txt)
>         n="${name#codereview-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/codereview-large.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --src "$WL/corpus/py-rich" \
>           --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       thinking-hard.txt)
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/thinking-hard.prompt.txt" \
>           --target-tokens 0 --out "$GEN/thinking-hard.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*-v*.txt)  # variant set: build the base with enough --variants (writes v1..vK)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%%-v*}"; k="${name##*-v}"; k="${k%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" \
>           --variants "$k" --out "$GEN/agentic-$n.txt" ;;
>       agentic-*.txt)
>         n="${name#agentic-}"; n="${n%.txt}"
>         python3 "$WL/build_prompt.py" --task "$WL/tasks/agentic-implement.task.md" \
>           --src "$WL/corpus/ts-agentic-code-runner" --target-tokens "$n" --out "$GEN/$name" ;;
>       *)
>         echo "FIXTURE-UNKNOWN: no build recipe for '$name' — build it by hand (see" \
>              "bench/workloads/README.md) and re-run" >&2; exit 1 ;;
>     esac
>     [ -f "$GEN/$name" ] || { echo "FIXTURE-BUILD-FAILED: $name" >&2; exit 1; }
>   done; }

< hard-fails it and a stronger one scores higher; recalibrate with `calibration*.jsonl`), and the corpus

> Start from a **new campaign dir** — a new campaign is a dir with `configs.jsonl` + `campaign.env` +
> `inputs/tasks.jsonl`, run with `bench/quality/run_capture.sh <dir>`. Keep the shared tools under
> `bench/quality/` (`ts-harness/`, `score_typescript.py`, `build_context.py`, `make_charts.py`,
> `run_capture.sh`) as-is. Change: `configs.jsonl` (your models/knobs) + `campaign.env` (this campaign's
> identity/defaults), `inputs/tasks.jsonl` + `bench/quality/ts-harness/tasks/*` (your tasks — follow the
> admission rule: a task is only kept if a weak model hard-fails it and a stronger one scores higher;
> recalibrate with `inputs/calibration*.jsonl`), and the corpus in `bench/quality/ts-harness/corpus/` if
> you want different deep context. Re-run `python3 bench/quality/score_typescript.py selftest` to confirm
> the grader still discriminates before spending GPU time.

18a25,38
> def jld(D, name):
>     """Rows of `name` under D, or under its split roots (results/ = graded results, tmp/ = raw).
>     A flat legacy run dir (or the synthetic test layout) is searched first, so a single dir and a
>     split campaign dir read identically."""
>     for root in (D, os.path.join(D, "results"), os.path.join(D, "tmp")):
>         p = os.path.join(root, name)
>         if os.path.exists(p):
>             return jl(p)
>     return []
> def results_root(D):
>     """results/ for a split campaign dir; D itself for a flat legacy run dir (or a fresh one
>     that has not created results/ yet)."""
>     r = os.path.join(D, "results")
>     return r if os.path.isdir(r) else D

---
> Q="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"           # bench/quality — the shared tools
> D="${1:-${CAMPAIGN_DIR:-}}"
> [ -n "$D" ] || { echo "usage: run_capture.sh <campaign_dir>  (or CAMPAIGN_DIR=<dir>)" >&2; exit 2; }
> D="$(cd "$D" 2>/dev/null && pwd)" || { echo "run_capture: campaign dir not found: ${1:-${CAMPAIGN_DIR:-}}" >&2; exit 2; }
> REPO="$(git -C "$D" rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null)"
> [ -n "$REPO" ] || REPO="$(cd "$Q/../.." && pwd)"
> SERVE="$REPO/stack/serve/llamacpp/serve_llamacpp.sh"
> ENV_FILE="$D/campaign.env"
> [ -f "$ENV_FILE" ] || { echo "run_capture: missing $ENV_FILE — campaign identity + defaults (one KEY=value per line; see an existing campaign)" >&2; exit 2; }
> # --- campaign.env: this campaign's defaults (the values its old driver copy patched in). A
> #     variable already set in the environment (a caller override) always wins, so only
> #     currently-UNSET keys are assigned. Lines are plain KEY=value (a whole-line comment, or a
> #     trailing "  # comment" on a value line, is stripped); no command substitution is ever run. ---
> while IFS= read -r _line; do
>   case "$_line" in ''|'#'*) continue;; esac
>   [[ "$_line" == *=* ]] || { echo "run_capture: bad line in $ENV_FILE (want KEY=value): $_line" >&2; exit 2; }
>   _key=${_line%%=*}; _val=${_line#*=}
>   [[ "$_key" =~ ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$ ]] || { echo "run_capture: bad key in $ENV_FILE: $_line" >&2; exit 2; }
>   if [[ "$_val" =~ ^(.*[^[:space:]])[[:space:]]+#.*$ ]]; then _val="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"; fi   # strip trailing comment
>   while [[ "$_val" =~ [[:space:]]$ ]]; do _val="${_val%[[:space:]]}"; done                     # rstrip
>   [ -n "${!_key+x}" ] || printf -v "$_key" '%s' "$_val"
> done < "$ENV_FILE"
> : "${TITLE:=$(basename "$D")}"

> def run_main(mod, argv, env=None):
>     """Run mod.main() with sys.argv = [mod_name] + argv and an optionally-patched CAMPAIGN_DIR."""
>     old_argv = sys.argv
>     old_env = os.environ.get("CAMPAIGN_DIR")
>     sys.argv = [os.path.basename(mod.__file__)] + argv
>     if env is not None:
>         os.environ["CAMPAIGN_DIR"] = env
>     try:
>         with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
>             mod.main()
>     finally:
>         sys.argv = old_argv
>         if env is not None and old_env is None:
>             os.environ.pop("CAMPAIGN_DIR", None)
>         elif old_env is not None:
>             os.environ["CAMPAIGN_DIR"] = old_env

> # ------------------------------------------------------------------ --campaign split layout
> class TestCampaignSplit(unittest.TestCase):
>     """--campaign (or $CAMPAIGN_DIR): raw read/written from/to tmp/, results written to results/,
>     fixtures read from inputs/; an explicit path flag always wins over the default."""
>     def test_campaign_defaults_resolve_to_split_layout(self):
>         r = campaign_with_data()
>         run_main(A, ["--campaign", r.d])
>         # results -> <dir>/results/
>         self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(r.results, "summary.md")))
>         self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(r.results, "summary.json")))
>         j = json.load(open(os.path.join(r.results, "summary.json")))
>         # scores read from results/, raw (outputs/vram) from tmp/, calib fixtures from inputs/
>         self.assertEqual(j["cells"]["c1"]["ts_pct"], 80.0)      # results/scores_typescript.jsonl
>         self.assertEqual(j["cells"]["c1"]["peak_vram"], 20000)  # tmp/vram.jsonl
>         self.assertAlmostEqual(j["bands"]["haiku"], 70.0)       # inputs/calibration.jsonl
>     def test_campaign_dir_env_default(self):
>         r = campaign_with_data()
>         run_main(A, [], env=r.d)                                # CAMPAIGN_DIR env, no --campaign flag
>         self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(r.results, "summary.md")))
>     def test_by_task_default_writes_results(self):

< **The driver decides.** On **Vulkan/RADV** (b9950) deep Gemma is non-viable: `llama-server` dies mid-prefill with `decode() failed: vk::Queue::submit: ErrorDeviceLost` → `vk::DeviceLostError`, a GPU device-loss that **crashes the whole Ubuntu desktop** — MEASURED at n_tokens **~65.5k @ctx163840** and **~24.5k @ctx140000** (lowering ctx doesn't help; no `allocation failed` line — a RADV hang, not OOM, though a concurrent 31 GiB stray server turns it into a hard OOM). On **ROCm/HIP** (`bench/llamacpp/llama-server` b1-049326a, `HSA_OVERRIDE_GFX_VERSION=12.0.1`) the **same q8_0/ctx150000 config runs clean end-to-end** — MEASURED: prefilled the full ~140k context and generated all 5 replies, **no device-loss, no GUI crash**, past the exact ~65k point where Vulkan died. So the crash is a **RADV driver bug, not a hardware/memory limit**. The stale repo "ROCm ~92× slower" figure did NOT hold: ROCm prefill was ~650→200 tok/s (decays with depth). The real ROCm cost is **decode: ~11.6 t/s (~6× slower than Vulkan's 70.9)** and long TTFA (351.6s).
